- **inspetor-de-terreno-da-junta**: Nasce ANTES de qualquer junta e a libera ou bloqueia. Não julga o mérito da entrega — julga se o TABULEIRO está limpo e justo para o voto: árvore sem mutação viva, isolamento por jurado (worktree próprio + cluster descartável), insumos do briefing presentes, inelegibilidade dos papéis conferida, fatia S0 executada, baseline honesto medido e plano de quórum declarado. Poder de VETO sobre o START da junta. Dorme quando a junta começa. ([thiagodorgo/ERP_Techsolutios](https://github.com/thiagodorgo/ERP_Techsolutios/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/inspetor-de-terreno-da-junta.md) · 2 copies)

Given a list of approved doc edits, it writes each one via the bundled inspire-docs write_doc tool — which is path-bounded to the project's docs directory — and reports what landed. Write-restricted by design: its only write path is write_doc, so it physically cannot edit files outside the docs directory or touch the inspiration corpus. ([skqr/claude-inspire-plugin](https://github.com/skqr/claude-inspire-plugin/blob/HEAD/inspire/agents/inspire-applier.md))
- **inspire-reader**: Reads a single non-YouTube web page for the /inspire skill — pulls its main text via the bundled inspire-content MCP and evaluates the page against the project it's running in (what it's about, what we can learn, what's directly applicable). Read-only and minimal-egress by design: it reads untrusted third-party page text, so it has no shell, cannot write or edit files, and its only network reach is the SSRF-guarded webpage tool. ([skqr/claude-inspire-plugin](https://github.com/skqr/claude-inspire-plugin/blob/HEAD/inspire/agents/inspire-reader.md))
- **inspire-scribe**: Writes the corpus files /inspire has already composed. Given the exact path and full contents of each inspiration note (and the corpus README), it writes them via the bundled inspire-docs write_note tool — which is path-bounded to the inspiration corpus (&lt;docs&gt;/inspiration/) — and reports what landed. Write-restricted by design: its only write path is write_note, so it physically cannot touch the project's own docs or anything else outside the corpus. ([skqr/claude-inspire-plugin](https://github.com/skqr/claude-inspire-plugin/blob/HEAD/inspire/agents/inspire-scribe.md))
- **inspire-watcher**: Watches a single YouTube video for the /inspire skill — pulls its transcript via the bundled inspire-content MCP and evaluates the video against the project it's running in (what it's about, what we can learn, what's directly applicable). Read-only and no-egress by design: it reads untrusted third-party transcript text, so it has no shell, cannot write or edit files, and cannot reach the network beyond the transcript tool. ([skqr/claude-inspire-plugin](https://github.com/skqr/claude-inspire-plugin/blob/HEAD/inspire/agents/inspire-watcher.md))

- **intake-interviewer**: Read-only analyst for the course-factory intake step: reads a rough COURSE_SPEC.md plus the insights/ digest and reports the resolved COURSE_BRIEF.md fields it can confidently derive, an explicit list of blocking questions for any missing or ambiguous required field (never a fabricated default), and a recommended archetype-profile + module selection. USE WHEN running `/course-intake` or otherwise turning a rough course spec into an intake report. Recommends; does not write COURSE_BRIEF.md itself — the orchestrating session asks the blocking questions and authors the file. - **intake-leader**: The standing intake seat: mints durable records from raw reports, ideas, and work items by applying the preloaded file-bug/file-feature/file-task/file-leftovers procedures inline — capture, classify, dedup, record, stop. Dispatched with one seed (or a batch) plus the target repo root; typically spawned as a long-lived sibling named INTAKE, or dispatched one-shot. Intake ONLY, structurally: its tool wall omits Agent and Skill, so it cannot dispatch builds or investigations — a bug record reports its resume command instead. NOT for triaging already-filed external GitHub items on a schedule (har… ([kimgranlund/claude-plugins](https://github.com/kimgranlund/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/intake-leader.md))

- **integration-reviewer**: 통합 리뷰어(QA). 데이터 모델-마이그레이션-성능-보안 간의 정합성을 교차 검증하고, 운영 준비성을 평가한다. ([sideprojectmate/sideProjectMate](https://github.com/sideprojectmate/sideProjectMate/blob/HEAD/src/lib/data/harness-100/ko/19-database-architect/.claude/agents/integration-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **integration-reviewer**: Runtime integration validator — read-only. Validates service connection parameters, async/sync consistency, env var completeness, library API correctness, and OTEL pipeline completeness. Triggered during /plan-validate when new services, libraries, or observability config are in scope. ([ArogyaReddy/https-github.com-FlorianBruniaux-claude-code-ultimate-guide](https://github.com/ArogyaReddy/https-github.com-FlorianBruniaux-claude-code-ultimate-guide/blob/HEAD/examples/agents/integration-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 10 copies)
- **integration-reviewer**: Subagente del skill story-code-review. Valida que los componentes implementados respetan la arquitectura definida en design.md y las convenciones de constitution.md. Si testcases.md está disponible, verifica la trazabilidad de referencias de diseño (D-N) en los casos de prueba. Escribe su informe parcial a .tmp/story-code-review/{story_id}/integration-report.md con el formato de contrato definido. Invocado exclusivamente por el orquestador story-code-review — no invocar directamente. ([dariopalminio/agile-sddf](https://github.com/dariopalminio/agile-sddf/blob/HEAD/skills/story-code-review/agents/integration-reviewer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **integration-reviewer**: Reviews an integration diff against the codebase's own structural baseline and against the ticket's acceptance criteria. Detects the pipeline family from the changed files, pattern-matches the reference partner declared in integration-profile.yml, walks the registration/mapping/idempotency/test checklist, and returns a structured report with a pass/fail verdict. Use before opening any PR that touches integration code — after quorum-new-integration scaffolds a partner, or after any edit under the integrations root. ([enriquemoya/quorum-plugins](https://github.com/enriquemoya/quorum-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/quorum-integration-kit/agents/integration-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **integration-reviewer**: Use as the mandatory final gate before commit on cross-domain work, after each domain verifier signs off: a read-only check of the assembled feature against the frozen contract, the seams, build, tests, migration and deploy safety. Do NOT use to fix, or for single-stack work. ([envoydev/alfred-code](https://github.com/envoydev/alfred-code/blob/HEAD/stack/agents/integration-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **integration-reviewer**: Phase 6: the last gate, and the only whole-result one — review the assembled diff on the integration branch against the merged spec and the original request. Every earlier reviewer checked exactly one task against its own criteria by design. Findings go to the run summary, never back into a build loop. ([febradc-github/yolo-dag](https://github.com/febradc-github/yolo-dag/blob/HEAD/agents/integration-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **integration-reviewer**: Performs final cross-layer review after parallel work or a substantial feature, checking contracts, dependencies, docs, tests, and merge coherence. ([Gaurav890/everything-agentic-engineering](https://github.com/Gaurav890/everything-agentic-engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/integration-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **integration-reviewer**: Reviews a PR as part of the whole system, not just its latest commits - how it fits with main and with other open PRs, whether its "verified" claims hold up, and, from round 2 on, whether review and fixes are going in circles. Used by a project's Manager session at the bar before a PR reaches the user; complements code-reviewer, which reviews one diff. ([jelbirt/LLM-Skill-Build](https://github.com/jelbirt/LLM-Skill-Build/blob/HEAD/agents/integration-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **integration-reviewer**: Reviews the staged Butlery diff AS A WHOLE for cross-file breakage — MVVM layering violated across files, a model changed without its generated code, Dart/Firestore/Cloud-Functions field drift, and duplication introduced across the batch. Run before committing any .dart change, and before pushing a range of batch commits. ([Malingisslen/butlery](https://github.com/Malingisslen/butlery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/integration-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **integration-reviewer**: Gate E reviewer (after the join). Clean-context editor that judges the three accepted branches together - script and content-pack, measured voice and mix plan, storyboard and style frames - as the viewer's planned experience, and catches every cross-branch mismatch before full build. New agent every round. ([nanxnguyen/remotion-ai-agentic-studio](https://github.com/nanxnguyen/remotion-ai-agentic-studio/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/integration-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **integration-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that checks frontend-backend integration — contract adherence, real HTTP path, error propagation, UI states. ([niels-emmer/myace](https://github.com/niels-emmer/myace/blob/HEAD/collections/additional/fullstack/agents/integration-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **integration-reviewer**: The last review of a finished change — its blast radius, and whether the whole solution would be smaller had the codebase been built with this feature from the start. Spawned after the other reviewers' findings are dealt with (CLAUDE.md "Review"); not for direct use. The prompt must include the base branch and the issue(s) or the goal of the change. ([synesenom/the-other-door](https://github.com/synesenom/the-other-door/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/integration-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **integration-reviewer**: Review wiring, runtime behavior, and cross-boundary integration against the exact commit. ([acartag7/claude-code-model-gateway](https://github.com/acartag7/claude-code-model-gateway/blob/HEAD/plugins/model-gateway/agents/integration-reviewer.md))
- **integration-reviewer**: Compatibility reviewer alias for a wiring-heavy slice. When chosen, it replaces the normal reviewer; it is never added as a panel seat. ([acartag7/engineering-os](https://github.com/acartag7/engineering-os/blob/HEAD/plugins/engineering-os/agents/integration-reviewer.md))
- **integration-reviewer**: Reviews cross-boundary consistency — daemon API ↔ shared-schemas ↔ desktop client, WS event contracts, Makefile/CI truthfulness, docs vs implementation drift. Use before marking a phase complete. ([adityafilesx/OmniMac](https://github.com/adityafilesx/OmniMac/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/integration-reviewer.md))
- **integration-reviewer**: Reviews the whole system at the end of each backlog phase and at the very end. Looks for inconsistencies between parts, duplicated patterns, rule drift and an uneven look. Use for tasks R-F1..R-F9 and R-FINAL. ([amilcar-neto/leverage-gta](https://github.com/amilcar-neto/leverage-gta/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/integration-reviewer.md))
- **integration-reviewer**: Read-only audit of publishing, import, analytics, webhook, and other provider adapters. ([Architect4Hire/CreatorPantry](https://github.com/Architect4Hire/CreatorPantry/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/integration-reviewer.md))
- **integration-reviewer**: Whole-change review for integration and architectural failures that no single-file reader can see - contract drift between caller and callee, ordering and durability violations across a boundary, workspace-scoping lost in a handoff, schema/model/migration divergence, configuration declared but never read. Use alongside the per-shard code-reviewer passes in the first-line-review skill, or any time a change spans more than one package, service, or layer. ([boongh/thought-capture](https://github.com/boongh/thought-capture/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/integration-reviewer.md))
- **integration-reviewer**: Pre-implementation architect review and pre-PR integration gates. ([ByteDeskAI/bytedesk-marketplace](https://github.com/ByteDeskAI/bytedesk-marketplace/blob/HEAD/platform-dev/agents/integration-reviewer.md))
